- issue-009: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Applicable lenses consistently support the high-severity authority-collapse issue. Because it can affect promise, picking, accounting, and reconciliation authority, the target ontology should not ship with the collapsed meaning. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-84,115-116 Source finding context: `quantity_on_hand`가 WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고라는 서로 다른 의미의 수량을 하나의 속성명으로 합쳐 권위 의미를 잃는다. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서에서 재고 수량은 어떤 시스템과 어떤 기준이 권위인지가 핵심 의미다. 물리적 보유량과 회계상 수량은 불일치할 수 있다고 문서가 직접 인정하므로, 하나의 `quantity_on_hand`로 모델링하면 동일 이름의 다른 의미를 구분하지 못한다. Source finding context: 재고 수량 개념을 권위와 기준별로 분리한다. 예: `physical_on_hand_qty`(WMS 권위, 창고 실물 기준), `accounting_on_hand_qty`(ERP 권위, 회계 기준), 그리고 두 값의 조정/대사 이벤트 또는 기준 시점을 별도 개념으로 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 재고 권위와 의미를 정합하게 정의하는 목적. Source finding context: WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고가 불일치하는 상태에서 통합자가 `quantity_on_hand`를 단일 의미의 현재 보유 수량으로 해석할 때. Source finding context: 주문 가능 여부, 피킹 가능 수량, 회계 조정 수량이 같은 필드로 오인되어 재고 약속, 출고, 대사 로직의 권위가 흔들린다. Source finding context: 서로 다른 권위 기준의 재고 수량을 하나의 속성명과 정의로 표현했다. Source finding context: `quantity_on_hand`는 '현재 보유 수량'으로 명명되어 단일 현재 재고처럼 보인다. Source finding context: 동일 설명 안에서 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가진다고 하여 필드가 둘 이상의 권위 값을 담는다고 밝힌다. Source finding context: 따라서 속성명이 실제 의미를 정확히 반영하지 못하고 WMS physical stock과 ERP accounting stock의 동음이의어적 충돌을 만든다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-010: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The resolution keeps this as a standalone high-severity temporal/semantic issue while noting it is a specific manifestation of issue-002. Its direct oversell and allocation-risk condition makes it target-blocking despite the shared root context. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty and inventory allocation rule Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86-90,111-112 Source finding context: `available_qty`가 매일 밤 저장되는 스냅샷인데 일중 주문 할당의 가용 재고로 쓰여 시간 의미가 이름과 맞지 않는다. Source finding context: `available_qty`라는 이름은 할당 시점의 가용 수량을 뜻하지만, 정의상 값은 야간 스냅샷이다. 시간 기준(`as_of`, snapshot time, reservation cutoff)이 없어서 '현재 가용'과 '스냅샷 가용'이 같은 개념으로 합쳐진다. Source finding context: 속성명을 `available_qty_snapshot`처럼 시간성을 드러내거나, `as_of_at`, 집계 기준, 예약/할당 반영 범위를 함께 모델링한다. 일중 할당은 실시간 ATP/예약 원장 등 별도 권위 개념을 참조하게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: 주문·재고 개념의 시간성과 운영 위험을 통합 기준 문서 안에서 명확히 하는 목적. Source finding context: 일중 주문/예약/출고로 재고가 변한 뒤에도 allocation rule이 전일 야간 스냅샷 `available_qty`를 현재 가용 재고로 사용할 때. Source finding context: 가용 재고 의미가 과거 스냅샷인지 현재 할당 가능량인지 불명확해져 과판매, 미할당, 시스템 간 재고 드리프트 위험을 만든다. Source finding context: 시간 기준이 있는 스냅샷 값을 시간 기준 없는 현재 가용 재고 이름으로 노출했다. Source finding context: `available_qty`는 '가용 수량'으로 정의되어 현재 할당 가능한 수량처럼 보인다. Source finding context: 동일 속성 설명은 이 값이 매일 밤 배치로 저장되는 스냅샷이라고 한다. Source finding context: 재고 할당 규칙은 일중 주문도 이 스냅샷 값을 읽는다고 하여 현재 가용 의미와 스냅샷 의미의 불일치를 운영 경로에 연결한다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-004: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Applicable lenses consistently support the authority and audit root at medium severity. It is not the primary happy-path blocker, but it must be addressed before the ontology can serve as a reliable integration governance baseline. Source finding context: Manual adjustment and multi-source authority evidence coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:43-45; logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:76-85; logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:110-112 Source finding context: The ontology lacks audit evidence and single-authority concepts for values managed across systems or manually adjusted. Source finding context: Coverage for WMS/TMS/ERP integration must include who can override operational facts, when, why, and which source wins. Without those concepts, the model cannot audit inventory reconciliation or ETA overrides, and downstream systems cannot distinguish carrier-reported facts from human-edited operational estimates. Source finding context: Add SourceAuthority/SourceSystem ownership for shared values and an Adjustment or OverrideEvent concept with actor_ref, occurred_at, reason_code, source_value, adjusted_value, approved_by, and affected field. Use it for ETA and inventory reconciliation at minimum. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: Authority, auditability, and operational risk control for WMS/TMS/ERP integration. Source finding context: When WMS and ERP quantities disagree, or when an ETA is manually changed after carrier data arrives, the ontology cannot identify the authoritative value or preserve evidence of the intervention. Source finding context: The concept baseline leaves high-risk operational controls outside the model, weakening dispute resolution, SLA explanations, and reconciliation audit trails. Source finding context: Shared and manually adjusted operational values are represented only as final scalar attributes, without authority or adjustment-event concepts. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand notes that WMS and ERP each hold values and are reconciled nightly to ERP, but the model has no authority or reconciliation event entity. Source finding context: Shipment.eta is a scalar datetime manually adjusted by operations, and the notes say carrier API values and manual adjustments are mixed into a final operations-judgment value.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-005: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The issue is consistently supported as a medium-severity lifecycle completeness gap. It affects common operational paths and should close before the ontology advances as a fulfillment operations baseline. Source finding context: Order, Shipment, and Tracking lifecycle coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:14-17; logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:39-42; logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:55-63; logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:106-108 Source finding context: Lifecycle models omit common terminal and exception paths needed for fulfillment operations. Source finding context: Coverage is incomplete for operational lifecycle states after or instead of successful delivery. Exceptions and post-delivery corrections are normal in fulfillment, and independent OMS/TMS/carrier status streams need a concept that can absorb them without making delivered the only terminal state. Source finding context: Extend lifecycle coverage with explicit terminal and exception states, plus lifecycle event concepts for cancellation, backorder, return/RMA, delivery failure, loss/damage, correction/reissue, and closure. Define how carrier TrackingEvent.exception maps into shipment/order lifecycle transitions. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-005 Source finding context: Status model completeness for order, shipment, and delivery operations. Source finding context: When a shipment fails, is returned, corrected after delivery, or a carrier reports an exception, the ontology has no first-class lifecycle path except successful delivery or independent unmapped tracking events. Source finding context: The integration baseline cannot consistently represent exception handling, reverse logistics, or post-terminal correction, making status reconciliation fragile across OMS, TMS, and carriers. Source finding context: Lifecycle coverage is success-path oriented and leaves exception/post-delivery states outside the core Order and Shipment status models. Source finding context: Order.fulfillment_status includes received, allocated, picking, shipped, delivered only. Source finding context: Shipment.status includes created, picked, in_transit, delivered only and states that delivered is final. Source finding context: TrackingEvent includes exception, but carrier events and internal statuses are managed independently and mapped by each integration.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-008: follow_up
  - rationale: The issue remains a distinct medium defect, but the resolution explicitly scopes it as downstream of issue-006 and preserves evolution's preference to fold it under that broader root. It should be carried with the DeliveryLeg redesign rather than treated as an independent top-level root. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / Shipment.status and DeliveryLeg inheritance Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input, `Shipment.definition`: "배송 구간(DeliveryLeg)도 Shipment의 일종이다."; `Shipment.attributes.status.values`: `[created, picked, in_transit, delivered]`; `Shipment.attributes.status.note`: "delivered가 최종 상태다."; `DeliveryLeg.is_a: Shipment`; `DeliveryLeg.definition`: "화물 경로의 한 구간(예: 창고→허브, 허브→고객)." Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` inherits a shipment-level final-state rule that is satisfiable for a whole shipment but logically over-constrains intermediate route legs. Source finding context: The conflict is between the necessary inheritance claim (`DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment`) and the necessary status-finality claim attached to `Shipment`. Under the explicit example `창고→허브`, the leg's completion condition is arrival at a hub, but the inherited final status is `delivered`; this forces the same entity type to use one terminal state for both leg-complete and shipment/customer-delivered. That weakens the document as a WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline because downstream integrations cannot consistently decide whether `delivered` closes a route segment or the customer shipment. Source finding context: Separate `DeliveryLeg` from `Shipment` inheritance, or split the state model into `Shipment.status` and `DeliveryLeg.status` with distinct terminal meanings such as `arrived_destination` for a leg and `delivered_to_customer` for a shipment. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for shipment and delivery concepts, especially status model consistency. Source finding context: A multi-leg route includes an intermediate leg such as warehouse-to-hub and uses inherited `Shipment.status.delivered` as the leg's terminal status. Source finding context: The same terminal status can represent two different closure events, so integrations may close the whole shipment when only a leg has completed, or fail to close a leg without implying customer delivery. Source finding context: The ontology models route segments as subclasses of whole physical shipments while placing an unqualified final delivery state on the parent type. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is declared as a subtype of `Shipment`. Source finding context: All `DeliveryLeg` instances inherit the `Shipment.status` enum and its unqualified final `delivered` rule. Source finding context: The inherited final state must also apply to intermediate route legs such as warehouse-to-hub legs.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-011: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The issue is consistently supported at medium severity as a semantic label conflict across OMS, TMS, and carrier authorities. It must be resolved before downstream integrations can rely on status equivalence. Source finding context: Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:19-22,44-47,63-66,111-113 Source finding context: `delivered`가 주문 상태, 화물 상태, 캐리어 이벤트에서 같은 표기로 쓰이지만 각 시스템이 독립 관리한다고 되어 있어 동음이의 의미가 구분되지 않는다. Source finding context: 주문이 delivered라는 것은 주문의 모든 필요한 fulfillment가 완료되었다는 OMS 판단일 수 있고, shipment delivered는 특정 화물의 도착 완료, tracking delivered는 캐리어가 보고한 이벤트 발생을 뜻한다. 같은 문자열이 서로 다른 권위와 완료 조건을 갖는데, 문서는 매핑을 각 연동에 맡겨 의미 기준을 제공하지 않는다. Source finding context: 상태 값을 네임스페이스 또는 별도 개념으로 구분하고, `carrier_delivered_event`, `shipment_delivery_completed`, `order_fulfillment_delivered`처럼 권위와 완료 조건을 명시한다. 세 모델 간 전이/매핑 규칙은 통합 기준 문서 안에서 표준화한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004 Source finding context: 주문·화물·배송 상태 모델의 의미 정합성과 시스템 간 precedence를 정의하는 목적. Source finding context: OMS, TMS, 캐리어 이벤트 중 하나의 `delivered`를 다른 시스템의 완료 상태와 동일시해 주문 완료, 고객 알림, 정산, SLA 판단을 수행할 때. Source finding context: 동일 표기 상태가 서로 다른 권위와 완료 조건을 갖기 때문에 배송 완료의 기준 시점과 책임 시스템이 통합마다 달라진다. Source finding context: 서로 다른 시스템 상태의 동일 표기 값을 의미적으로 네임스페이스화하거나 매핑하지 않았다. Source finding context: Order.fulfillment_status는 OMS 관리 주문 단위 진행 상태이며 `delivered` 값을 포함한다. Source finding context: Shipment.status는 화물 상태이며 `delivered`를 최종 상태로 둔다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type도 캐리어 보고 이벤트로 `delivered`를 포함하고, Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에 맡긴다. Source finding context: Integrity rule은 세 상태가 각 시스템에서 독립 관리된다고 하므로 동일 표기 값의 의미 동일성을 보장하지 않는다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-012: follow_up
  - rationale: The resolution retains this as a distinct medium aggregate-weight issue while tying it to the broader measurement-unit root in issue-003. It should be carried forward with measurement modeling, with closure possible during the next modeling stage. Source finding context: Sku.weight, Sku.dims, Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-38,40-49 Source finding context: SKU 중량·치수의 단위가 소스별로 다른데 Shipment.total_weight는 합계로 저장되어 단위 의미가 계산 결과에 보존되지 않는다. Source finding context: 중량 합계는 같은 측정 단위의 값끼리만 의미가 있다. SKU 단위가 kg/lb로 섞일 수 있는데 total_weight에 단위나 변환 기준이 없으면 이름이 가리키는 '총중량'과 실제 저장값의 의미가 불일치한다. Source finding context: 중량과 치수를 값+단위 구조로 모델링한다. `weight_value`, `weight_unit`, `dimension_unit` 또는 표준 단위 환산 규칙을 두고, `total_weight`도 단위와 계산 기준 시점을 함께 저장한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-005 Source finding context: 화물·재고 단위 개념의 정합성을 통합 기준 문서로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 서로 다른 창고나 소스 시스템의 SKU 중량이 하나의 Shipment.total_weight 계산에 섞일 때. Source finding context: 총중량 의미가 수치만 남고 단위 의미를 잃어 운임, 적재 제한, 운송사 연동에서 잘못된 물류 판단을 유발할 수 있다. Source finding context: 측정값의 단위를 속성 의미에 포함하지 않은 채 파생 합계 값을 정의했다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 숫자형이며 창고별 계량 관례에 따라 kg 또는 lb를 그대로 쓴다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 계산해 저장된다. Source finding context: 단위가 다른 숫자들이 합산될 수 있으므로 `total_weight`라는 이름의 총중량 의미가 실제 계산값과 어긋난다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-013: follow_up
  - rationale: The issue remains the distinct medium structural expression of the broader high-severity line-fulfillment issue-001. It should be tracked as the structural connectivity work item under the broader fulfillment-allocation fix. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / entities.OrderLine and relation fulfills Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: OrderLine attributes only define sku_ref and qty; relations includes only fulfills from Shipment to Order with note "주문 단위 관계이며 OrderLine 단위 매핑은 두지 않는다." Source finding context: OrderLine is not structurally connected to Shipment at fulfillment granularity. Source finding context: OrderLine is reachable from Order and Shipment is connected to Order, but the only fulfillment edge is order-level. The target declares itself as a concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across order, inventory allocation, shipment, and delivery. In that declared structure, line-level fulfillment is a necessary bridge between order content and physical movement; without it, partial shipments or split fulfillment cannot be represented as connected paths inside the ontology. Source finding context: Add a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, with refs to OrderLine and Shipment plus fulfilled quantity and timing/status fields as needed. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory allocation, shipment, and delivery. Source finding context: When one order has multiple lines, partial shipments, split shipments, or multiple delivery legs, the ontology cannot answer which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which Shipment/DeliveryLeg. Source finding context: This weakens structural completeness for integration because OMS order lines and TMS/WMS shipment execution cannot be reconciled through the model; downstream systems would need out-of-model joins or assumptions. Source finding context: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity while OrderLine has no outgoing or incoming fulfillment relation. Source finding context: OrderLine contains sku_ref and qty but no Shipment, DeliveryLeg, or fulfillment-allocation reference. Source finding context: The ontology's only declared relation connects Shipment to Order rather than Shipment to OrderLine. Source finding context: The relation note explicitly excludes OrderLine-level mapping.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-014: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The projection consistently supports this medium structural provenance issue. It enables the broader inventory audit and temporal concerns by disconnecting allocation values from their warehouse-level evidence, so it should close before the next stage. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / InventoryAggregate and InventoryRecord structure Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: InventoryRecord references Warehouse and Sku; InventoryAggregate references only Sku and has available_qty; integrity rule says allocation reads InventoryAggregate.available_qty. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate is structurally disconnected from the warehouse-level inventory records it summarizes. Source finding context: The structure contains InventoryRecord.wh_ref and sku_ref, and InventoryAggregate.sku_ref, but no aggregate-of, derived-from, or snapshot relation between them. Because allocation is declared to read InventoryAggregate.available_qty, the concept used for fulfillment decisions has no explicit structural path back to the warehouse records or warehouses that would execute the pick/ship flow. Source finding context: Add an explicit relation such as InventoryAggregate aggregates InventoryRecord, or model InventorySnapshot/AvailabilityByWarehouse with source InventoryRecord refs, aggregation time, and scope. If enterprise aggregate is intentionally independent, document its authoritative source relation explicitly. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002 Source finding context: Concept baseline for inventory allocation in WMS/TMS/ERP integration. Source finding context: When allocation decisions must be traced from enterprise available_qty to specific warehouse stock records for picking or reconciliation, the ontology has no connected structural path. Source finding context: This makes the allocation concept structurally incomplete: the value used to accept orders is disconnected from warehouse-level execution and reconciliation records, reducing actionability and auditability for integration. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate is modeled as a standalone SKU-level entity with no declared aggregate/source relation to InventoryRecord. Source finding context: InventoryRecord provides the warehouse/SKU inventory structure via wh_ref and sku_ref. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate provides SKU-level available_qty but only references Sku. Source finding context: No relation in the ontology connects InventoryAggregate to InventoryRecord; the only relation listed is fulfills from Shipment to Order.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-015: follow_up
  - rationale: The issue is consistently supported as a medium structural composition gap and is closely tied to the DeliveryLeg inheritance root in issue-006. It should be carried as part of the DeliveryLeg composition redesign. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / DeliveryLeg as Shipment subtype Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: Shipment definition says DeliveryLeg is also a kind of Shipment; DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment and has leg_seq/from_ref/to_ref; no attribute or relation links DeliveryLeg instances to a parent end-to-end Shipment. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg lacks a structural parent path to the shipment route it composes. Source finding context: The target intends to model shipment and delivery, and DeliveryLeg is defined as a route segment. A sequence number alone is not structurally meaningful without a parent route/shipment scope. Because DeliveryLeg inherits Shipment rather than being linked as a component, the model cannot represent which legs belong to the same shipment or reconstruct a route as connected segments. Source finding context: Model DeliveryLeg as a component of Shipment with a required shipment_ref or relation such as Shipment has_leg DeliveryLeg, keeping leg_seq scoped to that parent shipment. If legs remain subtypes, add a parent_shipment_ref for route composition. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003 Source finding context: Concept baseline for shipment and delivery path integration. Source finding context: When a Shipment has multiple route segments, the ontology cannot determine which DeliveryLegs compose that shipment or whether their sequence belongs to the same route. Source finding context: This breaks structural route completeness for TMS-style shipment execution and tracking because segment data cannot be reliably connected into one shipment movement. Source finding context: The model uses inheritance for DeliveryLeg but omits a composition relation from Shipment to DeliveryLeg. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is defined as a route segment with leg_seq/from_ref/to_ref. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is declared as is_a Shipment, making it a Shipment subtype rather than an explicit component connected to a parent Shipment. Source finding context: No relation or attribute connects DeliveryLeg instances to a parent end-to-end Shipment.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-016: follow_up
  - rationale: The projection explicitly classifies this as low severity and non-material, with no proven material failure condition. It is a useful improvement opportunity that should be watched or handled after the material DeliveryLeg composition issues are resolved. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / TrackingEvent linkage Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: TrackingEvent has shipment_ref to Shipment; DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment; no relation assigns events to a specific DeliveryLeg or leg sequence. Source finding context: TrackingEvent is not structurally connectable to a specific delivery leg. Source finding context: For a route with multiple DeliveryLegs, a carrier event such as arrived_hub or exception needs a structural attachment point to a leg or stop to preserve delivery-path context. The current single shipment_ref can point at the broad Shipment concept while the route segments are not composed under it, so event-to-leg connectivity is underspecified. Source finding context: Add an optional or required leg_ref from TrackingEvent to DeliveryLeg, or define event scoping rules plus a Shipment-to-DeliveryLeg composition relation that lets events be associated with a leg by route location and sequence. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-004
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`

### Consensus (6/6, core-axis mode)
- issue-001 (high): OrderLine fulfillment is materially incomplete because the ontology cannot trace each order-line quantity to the shipment or shipment quantity that fulfilled it.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: axiology
  - resolution accepted by: 4/4 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: coverage, structure, logic, semantics
  - remaining disagreement: 0/4 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage
  - issue statement: OrderLine fulfillment cannot be traced to the shipments or shipment quantities that actually fulfilled each line. Source finding context: OrderLine fulfillment coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26; logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104 Source finding context: OrderLine-to-Shipment fulfillment mapping is absent, so partial fulfillment coverage stops at the order level. Source finding context: For a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline, order fulfillment must reconstruct which quantities of which order lines were picked, shipped, split, or delayed. An order-level Shipment-to-Order relation cannot represent partial shipments, split shipments, substitutions, short picks, or line-level proof of fulfillment. Source finding context: Add a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept such as FulfillmentLine or ShipmentLine with order_line_ref, shipment_ref, shipped_qty, picked_qty, fulfilled_at, and status; keep the order-level relation as a derived summary if needed. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, freight, and delivery consistency. Source finding context: When an order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, or delayed/backordered lines, the ontology cannot identify which line quantity moved in which shipment. Source finding context: The model cannot support reconciliation between OMS order promises, WMS picking/packing, TMS shipment execution, and customer delivery evidence at the operational unit where fulfillment actually happens. Source finding context: Fulfillment is modeled only as a Shipment-to-Order relation, while OrderLine has no fulfillment allocation or shipment-line concept. Source finding context: OrderLine includes sku_ref and qty but no reference to a fulfillment, shipment, shipment line, delivery leg, or fulfilled quantity. Source finding context: The sole fulfills relation links Shipment to Order and explicitly states that OrderLine-level mapping is not included.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, freight, and delivery consistency.
  - failure condition: When an order has multiple lines, partial shipment, split shipment, or delayed/backordered lines, the ontology cannot identify which line quantity moved in which shipment.
  - impact: The model cannot support reconciliation between OMS order promises, WMS picking/packing, TMS shipment execution, and customer delivery evidence at the operational unit where fulfillment actually happens.
  - root hypothesis: Fulfillment is modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity, leaving no line-level allocation path from OrderLine quantities to shipment execution.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:20-26`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:100-104`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-001`, `finding-001.cause-001`, `finding-001.cause-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/axiology.findings.yaml`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.semantics`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: The declared purpose is a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline for order, inventory, freight, and delivery consistency. Without line-level fulfillment traceability, multi-line orders, partial shipments, split shipments, delayed lines, substitutions, and short picks cannot be reconciled between OMS promises, WMS picking and packing, TMS shipment execution, and delivery evidence.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with fulfillment being modeled only at Shipment-to-Order granularity. OrderLine carries sku_ref and qty, but no fulfillment allocation, shipment line, shipped or picked quantity, timing, or status path, so the model lacks the operational unit where fulfillment actually occurs.
  - causal path: Because OrderLine has no connection to a fulfillment or shipment-line concept, and the sole fulfills relation links Shipment to Order while excluding OrderLine-level mapping, shipment evidence stops at the whole-order level. When fulfillment is partial, split, delayed, or line-specific, the ontology cannot determine which line quantity moved in which shipment.
  - action: Add a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept, such as FulfillmentLine or ShipmentLine, linking order_line_ref, shipment_ref, shipped_qty, picked_qty, fulfilled_at, and status. Keep the order-level Shipment-to-Order relation only as a derived summary if needed, because the line-level allocation must be the authoritative path for reconciliation.

- issue-002 (high): Inventory quantities are modeled as current or nightly aggregate numbers without enough temporal, movement, reservation, lifecycle, and derivation structure. As a result, the ontology cannot reliably audit allocation decisions or evolve from nightly batch inventory sync to event-based synchronization while preserving the meaning of existing quantity fields.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: axiology
  - resolution accepted by: 5/5 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: coverage, evolution, structure, logic, semantics
  - remaining disagreement: 0/5 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage, evolution
  - issue statement: Inventory quantities lack the temporal and lifecycle structure needed to audit allocation decisions or evolve from nightly batch to event-based synchronization. Source finding context: InventoryRecord and InventoryAggregate temporal coverage Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:71-85; logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:106-107 Source finding context: Inventory quantities lack effective time, event history, and reservation lifecycle coverage. Source finding context: Inventory is time-dependent. Without as_of timestamps, transaction/reservation events, and allocation lifecycle states, the ontology cannot reconstruct inventory at order placement, explain oversell/undersell conditions, or reconcile WMS physical stock with ERP accounting stock across time. Source finding context: Introduce inventory temporal concepts such as InventorySnapshot(as_of), InventoryTransaction, Reservation/Allocation with lifecycle status, source_system, and quantity deltas. Define whether available_qty is derived from these records or an externally authoritative snapshot. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Inventory consistency and allocation correctness across WMS, ERP, OMS, and fulfillment operations. Source finding context: When inventory changes between nightly snapshots, or when users need to audit the inventory basis for an order accepted earlier in the day, the model has no historical or event-level representation to answer the question. Source finding context: The integration baseline cannot support inventory audit, allocation dispute resolution, or reliable reconstruction of past fulfillment decisions. Source finding context: Inventory is represented as current/snapshot quantities rather than as time-qualified records and lifecycle events. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is defined as current stock and lacks an as_of/effective-time attribute. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty is a nightly snapshot, yet intraday allocation reads it for new orders. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate temporal evolution Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` note says WMS and ERP each have values and ERP is reconciled by nightly batch; comment says no `as_of` concept; `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` note says daily nightly snapshot; integrity rule says intraday orders also read `InventoryAggregate.available_qty`. Source finding context: 재고 시간성 모델이 현재값/야간 스냅샷에 고정되어 이벤트 기반·실시간 할당으로 확장할 때 기존 재고 개념을 재구축해야 한다. Source finding context: 새로운 주문 채널이나 더 촘촘한 재고 동기화가 필요해지면 기존 `InventoryAggregate.available_qty`의 의미가 '야간 기준'에서 '실시간 기준'으로 바뀌거나 별도 필드가 추가되어야 한다. 시간 기준이 개념에 포함되어 있지 않기 때문에 과거 주문 당시 재고, 재처리, 감사, 재고 불일치 조정 규칙을 기존 데이터와 연결해 유지하기 어렵다. Source finding context: `InventorySnapshot`과 `InventoryMovement`/`Reservation`을 분리하고, 모든 재고 수량에 `as_of`, `source_system`, `snapshot_type`, `version` 또는 `effective_interval`을 둔다. 주문 할당은 최신 집계 필드가 아니라 특정 시점의 가용성 계산 또는 예약 이벤트를 참조하게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004 Source finding context: WMS/ERP 재고 권위와 주문 할당 기준을 통합 개념으로 안정적으로 유지하는 목적. Source finding context: 야간 배치에서 실시간 또는 이벤트 기반 재고 동기화로 전환하거나, 주문 당시 재고 상태를 감사/재처리해야 하는 조건. Source finding context: 시간 기준 없는 재고 숫자는 새 동기화 방식 추가 시 기존 값의 의미가 바뀌며, 주문 할당 로직과 감사 질의가 동일 개념 위에서 연속적으로 확장되지 않는다. Source finding context: 재고 수량 개념에 유효 시점과 변동/예약 이력을 포함하지 않았다. Source finding context: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` is a current quantity with WMS and ERP each holding values and nightly reconciliation to ERP. Source finding context: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` is a nightly snapshot, but intraday allocation also reads it. Source finding context: The target text explicitly notes the absence of an `as_of` concept for inventory point-in-time queries.
  - affected purpose: Inventory consistency, allocation correctness, and stable WMS/ERP inventory authority across fulfillment operations. Source finding context: Inventory consistency and allocation correctness across WMS, ERP, OMS, and fulfillment operations. Source finding context: WMS/ERP 재고 권위와 주문 할당 기준을 통합 개념으로 안정적으로 유지하는 목적.
  - failure condition: When inventory changes between nightly snapshots, when orders are allocated intraday, or when the system moves from nightly batch to real-time/event-based inventory synchronization, the model cannot reconstruct or preserve the basis of past inventory decisions. Source finding context: When inventory changes between nightly snapshots, or when users need to audit the inventory basis for an order accepted earlier in the day, the model has no historical or event-level representation to answer the question. Source finding context: 야간 배치에서 실시간 또는 이벤트 기반 재고 동기화로 전환하거나, 주문 당시 재고 상태를 감사/재처리해야 하는 조건.
  - impact: The integration baseline cannot reliably support inventory audit, allocation dispute resolution, or continuous evolution of inventory semantics without changing the meaning of existing quantity fields. Source finding context: The integration baseline cannot support inventory audit, allocation dispute resolution, or reliable reconstruction of past fulfillment decisions. Source finding context: 시간 기준 없는 재고 숫자는 새 동기화 방식 추가 시 기존 값의 의미가 바뀌며, 주문 할당 로직과 감사 질의가 동일 개념 위에서 연속적으로 확장되지 않는다.
  - root hypothesis: Inventory is represented as current or nightly aggregate quantities rather than time-qualified snapshots, movements, reservations, or effective intervals.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:71-85`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:106-107`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` note and inline comment`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` note`, `Embedded Materialized Input: integrity rule on allocation reading `InventoryAggregate.available_qty``, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-002`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-009`, `rel-002`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-002`, `finding-002.cause-001`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:71-79`, `finding-002.cause-002`, `logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml:81-85`, `finding-009.cause-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` note`, `finding-009.cause-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: first integrity rule`, `finding-009.cause-003`, `Embedded Materialized Input: inline comment under `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand``, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/axiology.findings.yaml`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.semantics`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-001`, `rel-004`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This is material because the declared purpose depends on inventory consistency, allocation correctness, and stable WMS/ERP inventory authority across fulfillment operations. If inventory changes between nightly snapshots or orders are allocated intraday, the model cannot reconstruct the inventory basis for an accepted order, explain oversell or undersell disputes, or reconcile WMS physical stock with ERP accounting stock over time.
  - root cause: The root cause starts at the inventory quantity model itself: InventoryRecord and InventoryAggregate represent current or nightly snapshot quantities, but do not encode as_of or effective time, movement history, reservation or allocation lifecycle state, or a clear derivation basis for available_qty. Because these qualifiers are absent at the source concept level, downstream allocation and audit logic has no stable time-qualified inventory authority to reference.
  - causal path: The causal path is that InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is a current stock value without an effective-time basis, while InventoryAggregate.available_qty is described as a nightly snapshot but is also read for intraday order allocation. That makes a scalar aggregate do two jobs: batch reconciliation and live allocation. When inventory changes during the day, the ontology has no event, reservation, snapshot timestamp, or effective interval structure to recover what quantity was valid at the decision point.
  - action: Fix this before relying on the model for allocation or release-quality inventory integration. Introduce InventorySnapshot, InventoryMovement or InventoryTransaction, and Reservation or Allocation concepts with as_of or effective time, source_system, lifecycle status, quantity deltas, and explicit derivation rules for available_qty. The aggregate-to-record provenance concern should remain aligned with the related issue-014 context, but it does not replace the broader temporal and lifecycle repair required here.

- issue-006 (high): DeliveryLeg must not remain a subtype of Shipment. The current inheritance makes a route segment carry the meaning and state contract of an end-to-end shipment, so the ontology should treat legs as shipment components with their own execution state model.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: axiology
  - resolution accepted by: 4/4 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: evolution, semantics, structure, logic
  - remaining disagreement: 0/4 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: evolution, semantics
  - issue statement: The ontology binds route segments and whole shipments through inheritance, mixing their meanings and making future shipment-state evolution global rather than local. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml: Shipment / DeliveryLeg evolution model Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `Shipment` definition says "창고에서 고객으로 가는 물리적 화물 이동. 배송 구간(DeliveryLeg)도 Shipment의 일종이다."; `DeliveryLeg` declares `is_a: Shipment`; `Shipment.status` values are `[created, picked, in_transit, delivered]`; `notes` says "국제/통관 배송은 이 온톨로지의 범위 밖으로 둔다." Source finding context: 배송 구간을 Shipment의 하위유형으로 고정해 통관·멀티모달·역물류 같은 새 이동 유형을 추가할 때 기존 Shipment 상태와 의미를 수정해야 한다. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment`이므로 구간은 Shipment의 속성과 상태 모델을 그대로 물려받는다. 동시에 `Shipment` 정의는 창고에서 고객으로 가는 전체 물리 이동이고, `DeliveryLeg` 정의는 그 이동의 한 구간이다. 이 두 개념의 변화 축이 다르기 때문에, 새 배송 도메인을 수용하려면 하위 구간 확장만으로 닫히지 않고 공통 부모의 상태 enum과 정의를 수정하게 된다. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서에서는 이런 부모 enum 수정이 기존 데이터 해석과 연동 매핑을 흔들 수 있다. Source finding context: `Shipment`를 주문 충족 화물 단위로 두고, 경로 실행 단위는 별도 `DeliveryLeg`/`TransportMovement` 엔티티로 분리한다. 상태도 `Shipment.status`와 `DeliveryLeg.status`를 분리하고, 통관·인계·반송 같은 미래 상태는 구간/이벤트 코드 확장 테이블이나 상태 매핑 개념으로 수용하게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 상태 모델을 장기간 확장 가능하게 유지하는 목적. Source finding context: 국제/통관 배송, 허브 간 다중 구간, 반송/교환, 운송사 인계 등 새 이동 유형을 온톨로지에 포함하려 할 때 `Shipment.status`와 `Shipment` 정의를 기존 데이터와 함께 수정해야 하는 조건. Source finding context: 통합 기준의 중심 개념인 Shipment의 의미와 상태 enum이 계속 변하면 기존 TMS/OMS/캐리어 연동 데이터의 상태 해석 연속성이 깨지고, 새 도메인 추가가 국소 확장이 아니라 전역 재분류 작업이 된다. Source finding context: 전체 화물 이동과 배송 구간이라는 서로 다른 변화 축의 개념을 상속 관계로 결합했다. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is declared as `is_a: Shipment`, so it inherits the parent Shipment contract rather than having an independently evolvable execution-state contract. Source finding context: `Shipment.status` is a closed enum with only created, picked, in_transit, delivered, and its note says delivered is final. Source finding context: The ontology explicitly excludes international/customs delivery, so adding that scope later would need states and meanings not represented in the inherited Shipment status set. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg.is_a Shipment Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40-58 Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg`를 `Shipment`의 하위 유형으로 둔 것은 경로 구간과 전체 화물 이동의 부분-전체 의미를 하위유형 의미로 잘못 표현한다. Source finding context: 배송 구간은 shipment의 구성 구간 또는 운송 실행 단위일 수 있지만, 정의상 '창고에서 고객으로 가는' 전체 물리 이동과 동일한 종류는 아니다. 이 상속은 구간별 상태/ETA/무게와 shipment 전체 상태/ETA/무게를 같은 의미로 만들 위험이 있다. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment`를 제거하고 `Shipment has_leg DeliveryLeg` 또는 `DeliveryLeg part_of Shipment` 관계로 바꾼다. 구간별 carrier, status, ETA가 필요하면 DeliveryLeg 고유 속성으로 정의한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 의미 정합성을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: TMS가 shipment 전체와 route leg를 별도 객체로 다루거나 구간별 상태/ETA를 통합할 때, leg가 shipment로 상속되어 전체와 부분의 의미가 섞이는 경우. Source finding context: 전체 shipment의 delivered/in_transit 의미와 개별 구간의 arrived/departed 의미가 같은 상태 모델로 오인될 수 있어 운송 추적과 ETA 집계가 부정확해진다. Source finding context: 부분-전체 관계로 정의된 DeliveryLeg를 하위유형 관계로 표현했다. Source finding context: Shipment는 창고에서 고객으로 가는 물리적 화물 이동으로 정의된다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 화물 경로의 한 구간으로 정의된다. Source finding context: 그런데 DeliveryLeg가 `is_a: Shipment`로 선언되어 구간이 전체 이동의 하위유형처럼 취급된다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for shipment, delivery, and long-term status-model evolution. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 주문·화물·배송 상태 모델을 장기간 확장 가능하게 유지하는 목적. Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 의미 정합성을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 제공하는 목적.
  - failure condition: When international/customs delivery, multi-leg hub routes, returns, carrier handoffs, or leg-specific ETA/status data are added, DeliveryLeg inherits the whole-Shipment contract instead of evolving as a route component. Source finding context: 국제/통관 배송, 허브 간 다중 구간, 반송/교환, 운송사 인계 등 새 이동 유형을 온톨로지에 포함하려 할 때 `Shipment.status`와 `Shipment` 정의를 기존 데이터와 함께 수정해야 하는 조건. Source finding context: TMS가 shipment 전체와 route leg를 별도 객체로 다루거나 구간별 상태/ETA를 통합할 때, leg가 shipment로 상속되어 전체와 부분의 의미가 섞이는 경우.
  - impact: Shipment meaning and status enums become unstable integration-wide, while route-level status and ETA semantics can be confused with whole-shipment delivery semantics. Source finding context: 통합 기준의 중심 개념인 Shipment의 의미와 상태 enum이 계속 변하면 기존 TMS/OMS/캐리어 연동 데이터의 상태 해석 연속성이 깨지고, 새 도메인 추가가 국소 확장이 아니라 전역 재분류 작업이 된다. Source finding context: 전체 shipment의 delivered/in_transit 의미와 개별 구간의 arrived/departed 의미가 같은 상태 모델로 오인될 수 있어 운송 추적과 ETA 집계가 부정확해진다.
  - root hypothesis: DeliveryLeg is modeled as a subtype of Shipment even though it is semantically a route segment or component of an end-to-end shipment.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Shipment` definition and attributes.status`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `DeliveryLeg` definition and `is_a: Shipment``, `Embedded Materialized Input: notes item `국제/통관 배송은 이 온톨로지의 범위 밖으로 둔다.``, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:40-58`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-006`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-013`, `rel-008`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-008`, `finding-006.cause-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `DeliveryLeg: is_a: Shipment``, `finding-006.cause-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Shipment.status` values and note`, `finding-006.cause-003`, `finding-013.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:40-41`, `finding-013.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:52-53`, `finding-013.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:52-58`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/axiology.findings.yaml`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.semantics`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-006`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: evolution, semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the ontology's purpose as a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline because Shipment is a central integration concept whose meaning and status enum need to stay stable over time. If leg, handoff, return, customs, or multimodal states are forced through Shipment.status, future domain additions become integration-wide reinterpretations rather than local extensions, and whole-shipment tracking semantics can be confused with route-leg semantics.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with the category choice: DeliveryLeg is modeled as is_a Shipment even though the evidence defines Shipment as the warehouse-to-customer physical cargo movement and DeliveryLeg as one segment of that movement. Because inheritance makes the leg inherit the parent Shipment contract, later status, ETA, and extension problems follow from this initial subtype decision.
  - causal path: The path is: DeliveryLeg inherits Shipment; Shipment.status is a closed enum of created, picked, in_transit, and delivered; and the ontology currently excludes international/customs delivery. When future route-level concepts such as carrier handoffs, hub legs, returns, customs, or leg-specific ETA/status are added, the inherited parent state model cannot express them locally, so Shipment's definition and enum would have to change and existing integrations could misread whole-shipment states as leg execution states.
  - action: Remove the DeliveryLeg is_a Shipment inheritance and model DeliveryLeg, or a TransportMovement equivalent, as a component of Shipment through has_leg or part_of. Keep Shipment as the order-fulfillment cargo movement, give DeliveryLeg its own status/ETA/carrier fields where needed, and support future customs, handoff, return, and route-event states through leg/event state extensions or mappings before broadening the shipment domain.

- issue-007 (high): Carrier status changes and new carrier onboarding cannot preserve a shared interpretation of shipment status because the ontology delegates carrier-event to internal-status mapping to individual integrations instead of owning that mapping as a shared concept.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, logic, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: structure
  - insufficient evidence lenses: axiology
  - resolution accepted by: 1/1 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: evolution
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: evolution
  - issue statement: Carrier status changes and new carrier onboarding cannot preserve a shared status interpretation because mapping authority lives outside the ontology. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml: TrackingEvent.event_type and Shipment.status integration boundary Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `TrackingEvent.event_type` values `[dispatched, arrived_hub, out_for_delivery, delivered, exception]` with note "캐리어별 상태 코드. 자사 Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다."; integrity rule says `Order.fulfillment_status`, `Shipment.status`, and `TrackingEvent.event_type` are independently managed. Source finding context: 캐리어 상태 변경이나 신규 운송사 추가 시 매핑 권위가 온톨로지 밖의 각 연동으로 흩어져 상태 모델의 연속성이 유지되지 않는다. Source finding context: evolution 관점에서 외부 표준 변경에 대한 내성이 핵심인데, 이 모델은 외부 캐리어 코드 변화의 흡수 지점을 공통 개념으로 두지 않는다. `TrackingEvent.event_type` 자체도 닫힌 enum이고, 매핑은 각 연동의 재량이므로 새 이벤트는 enum 수정과 여러 연동별 매핑 수정이 동시에 필요하다. 이는 TMS/캐리어 통합에서 가장 자주 변하는 상태 코드 영역의 확장성을 약하게 만든다. Source finding context: 캐리어 원천 이벤트 코드(`carrier_event_code`, `carrier_code_set_version`)와 내부 표준 이벤트/상태를 분리하고, 온톨로지에 버전 있는 `TrackingStatusMapping` 또는 상태 전이 규칙 개념을 추가한다. 신규 캐리어는 매핑 레코드 추가로 수용하고, 내부 `Shipment.status` enum 변경은 최소화한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: TMS와 캐리어 연동을 포함한 물류 통합에서 화물·배송 상태의 공통 해석 기준을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 신규 운송사 온보딩, 캐리어 상태 코드 개편, 예외 이벤트 세분화가 발생하는 조건. Source finding context: 상태 매핑 권위가 각 연동에 분산되면 같은 캐리어 이벤트가 연동마다 다른 Shipment 상태로 해석될 수 있고, 외부 표준 변경 시 기존 데이터와 규칙의 연속성을 온톨로지로 보장할 수 없다. Source finding context: 외부 캐리어 이벤트와 내부 Shipment 상태 사이의 매핑 개념을 온톨로지 권위로 모델링하지 않았다. Source finding context: `TrackingEvent.event_type` is modeled as a closed enum while also being described as carrier-specific status code. Source finding context: The mapping from carrier events to internal `Shipment.status` is delegated to each integration rather than represented as a shared ontology concept. Source finding context: The integrity rule further states the order, shipment, and tracking states are independently managed by OMS, TMS, and carriers.
  - affected purpose: Common interpretation of freight and delivery status across TMS and carrier integrations. Source finding context: TMS와 캐리어 연동을 포함한 물류 통합에서 화물·배송 상태의 공통 해석 기준을 제공하는 목적.
  - failure condition: When a new carrier is onboarded, carrier status codes change, or exception events are subdivided, each integration must decide mapping behavior independently. Source finding context: 신규 운송사 온보딩, 캐리어 상태 코드 개편, 예외 이벤트 세분화가 발생하는 조건.
  - impact: The same carrier event can map to different Shipment states across integrations, and external standard changes cannot be absorbed while preserving historical status meaning. Source finding context: 상태 매핑 권위가 각 연동에 분산되면 같은 캐리어 이벤트가 연동마다 다른 Shipment 상태로 해석될 수 있고, 외부 표준 변경 시 기존 데이터와 규칙의 연속성을 온톨로지로 보장할 수 없다.
  - root hypothesis: External carrier events and internal Shipment status mappings are delegated to individual integrations rather than modeled as ontology-owned mapping authority.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `TrackingEvent.event_type` note`, `Embedded Materialized Input: integrity rule on independent management of Order/Shipment/TrackingEvent states`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-007`, `finding-007.cause-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `TrackingEvent.event_type` values and note`, `finding-007.cause-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `TrackingEvent.event_type` note `자사 Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다.``, `finding-007.cause-003`, `Embedded Materialized Input: integrity_rules second item`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/axiology.findings.yaml`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.semantics`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-007`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-011`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-005`, `rel-012`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose of providing a common freight and delivery status interpretation across TMS and carrier integrations. When carrier codes change, a new carrier is onboarded, or exception events are subdivided, each integration can decide the mapping separately, so the same external carrier event may become different Shipment statuses and historical status meaning cannot be preserved consistently.
  - root cause: The root cause starts at the absence of an ontology-owned mapping authority between external carrier events and internal Shipment.status values. Because that authority is outside the ontology, downstream integrations become the effective source of truth for status interpretation.
  - causal path: The causal path begins with TrackingEvent.event_type modeled as a closed enum while also being described as carrier-specific. The mapping from those carrier events to Shipment.status is explicitly left to each integration, and the integrity rule says Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, and TrackingEvent.event_type are independently managed. Together, those choices make status interpretation distributed and fragile when carrier event vocabularies evolve.
  - action: Separate carrier source event codes from internal standard shipment states, then add a versioned TrackingStatusMapping or status-transition rule concept as ontology authority. New carriers and carrier code changes should be absorbed by adding or versioning mapping records, while changes to the internal Shipment.status enum remain minimized and controlled.

- issue-009 (high): InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is a material authority-collapse issue because it presents WMS physical stock and ERP accounting stock as one current on-hand quantity.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, logic, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: structure
  - insufficient evidence lenses: axiology
  - resolution accepted by: 1/1 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: semantics
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: The quantity_on_hand attribute loses authority meaning by naming two different inventory quantities as one current stock value. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:76-84,115-116 Source finding context: `quantity_on_hand`가 WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고라는 서로 다른 의미의 수량을 하나의 속성명으로 합쳐 권위 의미를 잃는다. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서에서 재고 수량은 어떤 시스템과 어떤 기준이 권위인지가 핵심 의미다. 물리적 보유량과 회계상 수량은 불일치할 수 있다고 문서가 직접 인정하므로, 하나의 `quantity_on_hand`로 모델링하면 동일 이름의 다른 의미를 구분하지 못한다. Source finding context: 재고 수량 개념을 권위와 기준별로 분리한다. 예: `physical_on_hand_qty`(WMS 권위, 창고 실물 기준), `accounting_on_hand_qty`(ERP 권위, 회계 기준), 그리고 두 값의 조정/대사 이벤트 또는 기준 시점을 별도 개념으로 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 재고 권위와 의미를 정합하게 정의하는 목적. Source finding context: WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고가 불일치하는 상태에서 통합자가 `quantity_on_hand`를 단일 의미의 현재 보유 수량으로 해석할 때. Source finding context: 주문 가능 여부, 피킹 가능 수량, 회계 조정 수량이 같은 필드로 오인되어 재고 약속, 출고, 대사 로직의 권위가 흔들린다. Source finding context: 서로 다른 권위 기준의 재고 수량을 하나의 속성명과 정의로 표현했다. Source finding context: `quantity_on_hand`는 '현재 보유 수량'으로 명명되어 단일 현재 재고처럼 보인다. Source finding context: 동일 설명 안에서 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가진다고 하여 필드가 둘 이상의 권위 값을 담는다고 밝힌다. Source finding context: 따라서 속성명이 실제 의미를 정확히 반영하지 못하고 WMS physical stock과 ERP accounting stock의 동음이의어적 충돌을 만든다.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for consistent inventory authority and meaning. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 재고 권위와 의미를 정합하게 정의하는 목적.
  - failure condition: WMS physical inventory and ERP accounting inventory disagree and an integrator interprets quantity_on_hand as a single current on-hand quantity. Source finding context: WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고가 불일치하는 상태에서 통합자가 `quantity_on_hand`를 단일 의미의 현재 보유 수량으로 해석할 때.
  - impact: Order promise, picking, accounting adjustment, and reconciliation logic can treat distinct authoritative quantities as one field, weakening inventory precedence and operational correctness. Source finding context: 주문 가능 여부, 피킹 가능 수량, 회계 조정 수량이 같은 필드로 오인되어 재고 약속, 출고, 대사 로직의 권위가 흔들린다.
  - root hypothesis: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand collapses WMS physical stock and ERP accounting stock into one attribute without a source-authority distinction.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:76-84`, `materialized-input.md:115-116`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-011`, `finding-011.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:81-83`, `finding-011.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:83`, `finding-011.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/round1/axiology.findings.yaml`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.semantics`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-009`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-003`, `rel-007`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-e3fc23c8/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: The ontology is meant to define consistent inventory authority and meaning across WMS/TMS/ERP integration. When WMS physical inventory and ERP accounting inventory can disagree, a single quantity_on_hand value lets integrators mistake order promise, picking availability, accounting adjustment, and reconciliation quantities for the same authoritative fact, weakening the baseline's operational and governance purpose.
  - root cause: The starting point is the InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand attribute itself: it collapses WMS physical stock and ERP accounting stock into one attribute without a source-authority or basis distinction. Because the ambiguity is introduced at the shared concept name and definition, downstream logic cannot reliably recover which quantity is authoritative for each use case.
  - causal path: The causal path starts with quantity_on_hand being named as a single current on-hand stock value. The same description then indicates that WMS and ERP each hold values for it, which means the field can carry more than one authoritative meaning. That creates a homonym conflict between physical warehouse stock and accounting stock, so the attribute loses the authority meaning needed for allocation, picking, reconciliation, and accounting decisions.
  - action: Fix this before release by splitting inventory quantities by authority and basis, for example physical_on_hand_qty for WMS-authoritative warehouse stock and accounting_on_hand_qty for ERP-authoritative accounting stock. Add reconciliation or adjustment-event concepts, including relevant basis or timing, so differences between the two quantities are modeled explicitly instead of hidden behind one field.

- issue-010 (high): InventoryAggregate.available_qty is a material high-severity temporal/semantic defect: it exposes a nightly inventory snapshot as unqualified current availability and allocation rules use it for intraday allocation as if it were current allocatable stock.
  - issue stance agreement: 3/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, logic, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 1/6

  - not applicable lenses: structure
